fist (`ج م ع:B005/m01`); physical casting (`ن ب ذ:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:1 هُمَزَةٍ (`ه م ز`) and لُّمَزَةٍ (`ل م ز`); 104:2 جَمَعَ (`ج م ع`); 104:4 يُنۢبَذَ (`ن ب ذ`)","synthesis":"Gathering and pressure converge in the clenched hand. The hand compresses, the prod transfers force, and the pushed object is finally thrown away, giving the surface casting a tactile prehistory.","ayah_refs":["104:1","104:2","104:4"]},{"parent_channel":"Denigration, Force, and Breaking","parent_semantic_invariant":"Concentrated pressure crosses from speech and gesture into propulsion, forced movement, and the loss of bodily or material integrity.","parent_surprising_reach":"The same force pattern extends through compressed articulation, a clenched palm, a prod or bow impulse, a charging mass, and an exhausted driven animal.","key":"C","name":"Crushing by Coalesced Mass","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Separate bodies gather momentum, overrun what lies before them, and leave brittle matter fragmented.","active_motifs":"brittle fracture and fragmentation (`ح ط م:B001/m01`); trampling herd or ravaging mass (`ح ط م:B006/m01`); coalesced charging force (`ج م ع:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:2 جَمَعَ (`ج م ع`); 104:4 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ and 104:5 ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`)","synthesis":"Crushing is not only a single impact. It can be the cumulative force of a herd, flood-like movement, or gathered charge whose parts become one destructive mass and reduce resistant material to fragments.","ayah_refs":["104:2","104:4","104:5"]},{"parent_channel":"Denigration, Force, and Breaking","parent_semantic_invariant":"Concentrated pressure crosses from speech and gesture into propulsion, forced movement, and the loss of bodily or material integrity.","parent_surprising_reach":"The same force pattern extends through compressed articulation, a clenched palm, a prod or bow impulse, a charging mass, and an exhausted driven animal.","key":"D","name":"Harsh Driving and Exhaustion","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A harsh driver forces living bodies onward until movement and faculties lose their edge.","active_motifs":"violent herding or driving (`ح ط م:B004/m01`); exhaustion of walker, mount, wind, or faculty (`ك ل ل:B001/m02`)","ayah_anchors":"104:1 كُلِّ (`ك ل ل`); 104:4 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ and 104:5 ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`)","synthesis":"The driver is named through the damage he causes: bodies are crowded and driven without relief, while motion, sight, hearing, or cutting power becomes spent and dull.","ayah_refs":["104:1","104:4","104:5"]},{"parent_channel":"Accumulation, Measure, and Claimed Permanence","parent_semantic_invariant":"Gathered quantities are counted, provisioned, extended, and treated as though numerical control could secure adequacy or duration.","parent_surprising_reach":"Quantity expands into total capacity, reserve logistics, calendrical spans, devotional fixation on property, and the return of worldly goods to breakable debris.","key":"A","name":"Hoarding, Calculation, and False Permanence","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Property is gathered and repeatedly counted, then mentally promoted from a reserve into a guarantor of lasting existence.","active_motifs":"gathering dispersed holdings (`ج م ع:B001/m01`); numerical enumeration (`ع د د:B001/m01`); accumulated property (`م و ل:B001/m01`); accounting and calculation (`ح س ب:B001/m01`); supposition (`ح س ب:B002/m01`); enduring permanence (`خ ل د:B001/m01`); devotion and object-making (`ء ل ه:B001/m01`); transient worldly goods (`ح ط م:B009/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:2 جَمَعَ (`ج م ع`), مَالًا (`م و ل`), and عَدَّدَهُ (`ع د د`); 104:3 يَحْسَبُ (`ح س ب`), مَالَهُ (`م و ل`), and أَخْلَدَهُ (`خ ل د`); 104:4-5 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ/ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`); 104:6 ٱللَّهِ (`ء ل ه`)","synthesis":"Counting converts possessions into an apparently closed system of security. The devotional branch sharpens the relation: wealth can be treated as an object of ultimate reliance, yet the crushing branch recasts its display and ornament as passing debris.","ayah_refs":["104:2","104:3","104:4","104:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Accumulation, Measure, and Claimed Permanence","parent_semantic_invariant":"Gathered quantities are counted, provisioned, extended, and treated as though numerical control could secure adequacy or duration.","parent_surprising_reach":"Quantity expands into total capacity, reserve logistics, calendrical spans, devotional fixation on property, and the return of worldly goods to breakable debris.","key":"C","name":"Prepared Reserves and Material Aid","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Supplies are measured, stored, and made ready so they can sustain a person, household, or force when need arrives.","active_motifs":"prepared equipment and reserves (`ع د د:B002/m01`); reinforcements and material aid (`م د د:B002/m01`); sufficient provision (`ح س ب:B003/m01`); measured allotment (`م د د:B006/m01`); small residual portion (`ن ب ذ:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:2 عَدَّدَهُ (`ع د د`); 104:3 يَحْسَبُ (`ح س ب`); 104:4 يُنۢبَذَ (`ن ب ذ`); 104:9 مُّمَدَّدَةٍ (`م د د`)","synthesis":"Enumeration becomes logistics: one prepares an outfit, reserve, weapon, food supply, or reinforcement, then portions it by measure. The tiny remainder and the sufficient store establish the lower and upper bounds of usable provision.","ayah_refs":["104:2","104:3","104:4","104:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Combustion, Heat, and Consumption","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is fed into heat, transformed by combustion or digestion, and carried from an external fire into bodily and emotional interiors.","parent_surprising_reach":"The fire scene includes fuel, hearth, cooking vessel, digestive action, inward anger, appetite, vomiting, summer heat, and drought.","key":"A","name":"Ignition, Fuel, Hearth, and Cooking","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Fuel is placed at a hearth, rapidly ignited, and used to heat, roast, or bake food before digestion completes the transformation.","active_motifs":"ignition and flame (`و ق د:B001/m01`); combustible fuel (`و ق د:B002/m01`); hearth or fire-place (`و ق د:B003/m01`); rapid kindling (`و ق د:B005/m01`); visible fire (`ن و ر:B002/m01`); heat and fire (`ف ء د:B001/m01`); roasting or baking (`ف ء د:B001/m02`); hearth and skewer implements (`ف ء د:B001/m03`); large cooking vessel (`ج م ع:B012/m01`); digestive action (`ح ط م:B008/m02`)","ayah_anchors":"104:2 جَمَعَ (`ج م ع`); 104:4-5 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ/ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`); 104:6 نَارُ (`ن و ر`) and ٱلْمُوقَدَةُ (`و ق د`); 104:7 ٱلْأَفْـِٔدَةِ (`ف ء د`)","synthesis":"The lexical branches assemble a complete combustion system: material fuel, a bounded hearth, ignition, flame, vessel, food, and digestive breakdown. External fire and internal digestion become parallel mechanisms for reducing and transforming matter.","ayah_refs":["104:2","104:4","104:6","104:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Combustion, Heat, and Consumption","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is fed into heat, transformed by combustion or digestion, and carried from an external fire into bodily and emotional interiors.","parent_surprising_reach":"The fire scene includes fuel, hearth, cooking vessel, digestive action, inward anger, appetite, vomiting, summer heat, and drought.","key":"C","name":"Gluttony and Emesis","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Appetite consumes beyond restraint until what entered the body rises back out.","active_motifs":"voracious eater (`ح ط م:B008/m01`); rising vomit (`ط ل ع:B011/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:4-5 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ/ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`); 104:7 تَطَّلِعُ (`ط ل ع`)","synthesis":"Consumption first resembles a fire that breaks food down, then reverses direction as the stomach expels what it could not contain. Rising changes from emergence into emesis.","ayah_refs":["104:4","104:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Combustion, Heat, and Consumption","parent_semantic_invariant":"Material is fed into heat, transformed by combustion or digestion, and carried from an external fire into bodily and emotional interiors.","parent_surprising_reach":"The fire scene includes fuel, hearth, cooking vessel, digestive action, inward anger, appetite, vomiting, summer heat, and drought.","key":"D","name":"Summer Heat and Drought","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Sustained seasonal heat dries the environment until a severe year breaks people, animals, and property.","active_motifs":"peak summer heat (`و ق د:B004/m01`); destructive drought-year (`ح ط م:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:4-5 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ/ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`); 104:6 ٱلْمُوقَدَةُ (`و ق د`)","synthesis":"Combustion expands to climate: heat becomes a season, the season becomes drought, and drought performs the same breaking action on living communities that impact performs on brittle matter.","ayah_refs":["104:4","104:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Enclosure and Structural Support","parent_semantic_invariant":"Space is bounded by doors, yards, pens, covers, restraints, walls, and load-bearing members that determine what remains inside and what can stand.","parent_surprising_reach":"The closed structure ranges from a sealed doorway to a mountain livestock pen, a bodily canopy, a hand-to-neck fetter, and a crowded sanctuary.","key":"E","name":"Crowded Sacred Gathering Place","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"People converge at a sacred gathering place whose wall or precinct becomes compressed by crowding.","active_motifs":"crowded or broken sanctuary precinct (`ح ط م:B007/m01`); pilgrimage gathering place or day (`ج م ع:B004/m02`)","ayah_anchors":"104:2 جَمَعَ (`ج م ع`); 104:4-5 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ/ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`)","synthesis":"Gathering supplies the human convergence, while the sanctuary branch gives it a built boundary vulnerable to pressure and breakage. Social assembly and architectural compression occupy one scene.","ayah_refs":["104:2","104:4"]},{"parent_channel":"Inquiry, Targeting, and Deliberate Action","parent_semantic_invariant":"Knowledge is sought or assembled, converted into intention, and then directed toward governance, reconnaissance, hunting, conflict, or formal exchange.","parent_surprising_reach":"Epistemic inquiry develops into administrative oversight, scouting, a concealed hunt, projectile mechanics, treaty renunciation, and sale by thrown token.","key":"E","name":"Declared Conflict and Tokenized Exchange","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Throwing an object or declaration changes a social relation by openly ending a pact or making a sale binding.","active_motifs":"treaty renunciation and open confrontation (`ن ب ذ:B002/m01`); sale concluded by thrown token or goods (`ن ب ذ:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:4 يُنۢبَذَ (`ن ب ذ`)","synthesis":"Casting becomes a formal sign rather than disposal. In one case it returns a pact and opens conflict; in the other it transfers goods and closes a transaction.","ayah_refs":["104:4"]},{"parent_channel":"Material Flow and Replenishment","parent_semantic_invariant":"A material moves through a channel or container to replenish, mix with, or extend another material system.","parent_surprising_reach":"The same replenishment mechanism governs rivers and wells, earth dams, steeped fruit drink, enriched animal water, and ink drawn from an inkwell into a pen.","key":"A","name":"Wells, Feeding Waters, and Earthworks","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water collects in a durable source, one flow replenishes another, and a dam redirects or holds the current against packed earth.","active_motifs":"perennial well or collected water (`ع د د:B004/m01`); water feeding another watercourse (`م د د:B003/m01`); dam across a stream (`ع م د:B013/m01`); rain-packed soil (`ع م د:B010/m01`); loose excavated earth (`ن ب ذ:B009/m02`); coalesced flood momentum (`ج م ع:B010/m02`)","ayah_anchors":"104:2 جَمَعَ (`ج م ع`) and عَدَّدَهُ (`ع د د`); 104:4 يُنۢبَذَ (`ن ب ذ`); 104:9 عَمَدٍ (`ع م د`) and مُّمَدَّدَةٍ (`م د د`)","synthesis":"The channel supplies source, flow, barrier, and construction material. Water gathers and extends itself through tributary feeding, while packed and excavated earth reshape that movement into a retained pool or redirected course.","ayah_refs":["104:2","104:4","104:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Material Flow and Replenishment","parent_semantic_invariant":"A material moves through a channel or container to replenish, mix with, or extend another material system.","parent_surprising_reach":"The same replenishment mechanism governs rivers and wells, earth dams, steeped fruit drink, enriched animal water, and ink drawn from an inkwell into a pen.","key":"B","name":"Steeped Drink and Enriched Animal Water","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A dry ingredient is cast into water and left to infuse, producing a drink or nutritive mixture.","active_motifs":"fruit steeped in a vessel (`ن ب ذ:B006/m01`); water enriched with meal or seed for animals (`م د د:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:4 يُنۢبَذَ (`ن ب ذ`); 104:9 مُّمَدَّدَةٍ (`م د د`)","synthesis":"Casting initiates mixture rather than rejection. Water receives fruit, flour, seed, or sesame, and the added substance extends plain liquid into a prepared drink or feed.","ayah_refs":["104:4","104:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Bodily Integrity and Attrition","parent_semantic_invariant":"Bodies move between intact strength and loss of integrity through pain, heart disease, aging, pressure wounds, suppuration, or altered skin.","parent_surprising_reach":"The body appears as chest and heart, aged mount, swollen saddle wound, vigorous youth, intact animal, and skin whose color shades into disease.","key":"B","name":"Aging, Pressure Wound, and Suppuration","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Age or repeated pressure weakens a body, damages tissue beneath an intact surface, and produces swelling or pus.","active_motifs":"senescence and emaciation (`ح ط م:B005/m01`); saddle or pressure wound (`ع م د:B009/m01`); accumulating wound discharge (`م د د:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:4-5 ٱلْحُطَمَةِ/ٱلْحُطَمَةُ (`ح ط م`); 104:9 عَمَدٍ (`ع م د`) and مُّمَدَّدَةٍ (`م د د`)","synthesis":"Attrition can be gradual rather than explosive. A body loses strength with age, sustained pressure crushes tissue under the skin, and the wound extends internally as swelling and discharge.","ayah_refs":["104:4","104:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Attachment, Exclusion, and Place","parent_semantic_invariant":"Persons and objects are positioned by clinging to a place, moving aside, or being cast beyond a social or spatial center.","parent_surprising_reach":"Spatial placement becomes a social relation encompassing residence, withdrawal, foundlings, dependents, and neglected animals.","key":"B","name":"Withdrawal to the Side","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A person leaves the center of activity and takes up a position at the side.","active_motifs":"moving or sitting apart (`ن ب ذ:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:4 يُنۢبَذَ (`ن ب ذ`)","synthesis":"Casting is internalized as self-placement: instead of being thrown away by another person, the person withdraws and occupies a marginal location.","ayah_refs":["104:4"]},{"parent_channel":"Attachment, Exclusion, and Place","parent_semantic_invariant":"Persons and objects are positioned by clinging to a place, moving aside, or being cast beyond a social or spatial center.","parent_surprising_reach":"Spatial placement becomes a social relation encompassing residence, withdrawal, foundlings, dependents, and neglected animals.","key":"C","name":"Cast-Off Dependents","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A dependent person or animal is rejected, left exposed, and made reliant on whoever receives or sustains it.","active_motifs":"rejection from consideration (`ن ب ذ:B001/m02`); abandoned child (`ن ب ذ:B007/m01`); neglected ewe (`ن ب ذ:B009/m01`); dependent, burden, or orphan (`ك ل ل:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"104:1 كُلِّ (`ك ل ل`); 104:4 يُنۢبَذَ (`ن ب ذ`)","synthesis":"Expulsion becomes a social condition. ## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
